At least 300 new effects in Making Money, 1,000 in Big City and 400 in Rush Hour took the manager's object. With the making's, the start's, the constructors' and the holders' comparisons on as well (Big City): 38,115, 1,842 and 292, with 38,606 makings of a record's effect and 47,869 starts, 0 divergences in all. Five mistakes put in on purpose were each reported on Big City: the index taken one too high (26,624 of 38,659 takings), the block at +0xCA4 (1,105 of 1,839 new ones), the reset's word left 0 (all 300), a compound effect's hold through slot 8 (457 of 1,851) and two free indices popped (25,577 of 36,947).

**The look-up by name and the compound effect's description.** The manager keeps its compound descriptions' names in a hash multimap at +0x98: the buckets are a vector of node pointers at +4, and a node holds the next node (+0), the name's text (+4 to +8) and the description's index (+0x10). The map's equal range (0x00586EF0, (out, name)) finds the bucket by the game's string hash (0x0051C9B0, h * 5 plus each byte as a signed number, modulo the bucket count). It gives the first node with that text, and the node after the last one: the next node, or the head of the next bucket that has one. The look-up (0x0058F5A0, (name) on the manager object) goes through those nodes as the map's iterator does, hashing a node's text again to find its bucket when the chain ends. Of their descriptions it answers the index of the one whose word +4 is the largest above 0 and not above the manager's word at +0xB90, or -1. A compound effect taking its description (0x005BFF80) keeps the description's first word at +0x28 and its words at +0x20 to +0x28 at +0x120 to +0x128, and clears +0x2C and +0x144. Each of its four blocks (at +0x34, +0x6C, +0xA4, +0xDC: a count of changes, a flag that it changed, a matrix, a place and a scale) that changed is made the identity's again, its count one more, as the decal's reset does with its one. Its vector of values at +0x12C is emptied.

`renderer.effects.lookup` replaces the look-up and the equal range, and `renderer.effects.compound_description` the description, written by hand in `src/core/effects_lookup.h`; the string hash stays the game's (code outside the effects calls it). All three only read, or write plain values, so compare mode runs the game's and then ours: the look-up's answers, the equal range's sixteen bytes and answer, and the compound effect's 0x14C bytes. Their detours are shared with the making's comparison. On the three test cities with the camera workout, clouds and water effects on (2026-10-14), 0 divergences: Making Money 4,767 look-ups, 4,849 equal ranges and 4,826 descriptions; Big City 12,576, 12,658 and 12,751; Rush Hour 6,211, 6,293 and 6,293. With the making's, the makers' and the start's comparisons on as well (Big City), 0 divergences in all. Four mistakes were put in on purpose on Big City, and two were reported: the fourth block left out (9,241 of 9,991 descriptions), and the limit taken as below rather than not above (1 of 11,601 look-ups, one where a description's word equalled the limit). Two went unseen, and say what the cities never have: a name with more than two descriptions next to each other in a chain (the equal range's walk past the second), and a description whose words at +0x24 and +0x28 differ.

**A decal's description** (0x00583480, (description) on the decal). A decal (0x124 bytes) keeps a copy of its description (0x64 bytes, the manager's table at +0x10C) at +0xC, made by the description's assign (0x005833F0, which copies its words and assigns its five vectors). It then keeps 1 over the description's speed (its +0xC) at +0x118, or 1 over 0.0001 when the speed is not above that. It lets go of the texture it held at +0x11C (0x008090E0) and draws three scales from the generator at 0x00B22C10: +0xF4 within the description's +0x4C of 1, +0xF8 within its +0x50 of 1, and +0xFC within its +0x54 of 0 when that is above 0, else 1. It sets +0x100, +0x104, +0x108 and +0x10C to 1, clears +0xE4, +0xE8, +0xF0 and the byte +0x110, and copies +0xF8 to +0xEC. With the description's flag 0x10 the byte +0x110 is set: the city's two sizes (its slots 0x168 and 0x164) go halved to +0xE8 and +0xE4, and the second whole to +0xEC.

`renderer.effects.decal_description` replaces it, written by hand in `src/core/effects_decal_description.h`; the assign and the texture's letting go stay the game's. It cannot run twice: the assign can allocate, and the letting go would be done twice. Compare mode runs the game's first, noting the assign with the bytes it changed in the decal (through a detour), then puts the decal and the generator back. Ours is replayed with the assign only noted and its changes made, and the letting go only noted. The generator's draws and the city's sizes are asked again for real: both only read, and the generator is put back between the two runs. The calls are compared (the game's letting go being of the texture the decal held), along with the decal's bytes and the generator's. Its detour is shared with the making's comparison. On the three test cities with the camera workout, clouds and water effects on (2026-10-14): Making Money 246 decals, Big City 12,352 and Rush Hour 202, with 0 divergences, and none with the making's, the makers' and the look-up's comparisons on as well. No decal in them held a texture or has the flag 0x10, and none has a speed at or below 0.0001, so those lines of ours have not run. Of five mistakes put in on purpose on Big City, the two on paths that run were reported: the spread's test taken as "not below 0" (12,664 of 12,986) and the second scale drawn within the first's spread (12,910 of 13,000). The three on paths that do not run went unseen: the flag's bit, the city's sizes, and the speed's floor.

**The constructors of a decal and a compound effect** (0x00583190, its description's 0x00582FE0, and 0x005C0150). The game's code stores some fields twice, first a global's value and then the final one; only the final values matter. A decal (0x124 bytes) gets its table of functions (0x00A9EE78), no holders (+4), the byte +8 cleared and its description at +0xC constructed. It also gets +0x70 set to -1 and its two blocks (+0x74 and +0xAC, each a count, a changed flag, a matrix, a place and a scale) cleared to the identity's. +0xE4, +0xE8, +0xF0 and the byte +0x110 are cleared, +0xEC, +0xF4 to +0x10C and +0x118 set to one, and +0x11C and +0x120 cleared. The decal's description (0x64 bytes) gets +0 and +4 cleared, the byte +8 cleared and +9 set, a speed of 5 at +0xC and four vectors of one float: +0x10 holding 0, +0x1C, +0x28 and +0x40 holding 1, filled by 0x00581F20. It also gets one vector of one three-float value at +0x34 (the floats at 0x00B0A214), +0x4C to +0x54 cleared, +0x58 one, and +0x5C and +0x60 the word at 0x00B22C08. A compound effect (0x14C bytes) gets its two tables of functions (0x00AA1170, and 0x00AA1148 at +4), no holders (+8), and an empty name at +0xC with eight bytes of room. +0x18 and +0x1C are cleared and +0x20 set to -1 (no description). The bytes +0x24 and +0x25 and the words +0x28 to +0x30 are cleared, its four blocks are cleared, and so are its records (+0x114) and +0x12C to +0x148.

`renderer.effects.ctors` replaces the three, written by hand in `src/core/effects_ctors.h`; the fill stays the game's. Each allocates, so compare mode runs ours on a copy of the memory and then the game's on the object itself. The two are compared byte for byte, except for the allocated pointers, which are compared by what they hold and by the sizes they give; ours' allocations are then freed. The detours of the decal's and the compound effect's constructors are shared with the makers' comparison. On the three test cities with the camera workout, clouds and water effects on (2026-10-14), 0 divergences: Making Money 106 decals, 214 decal descriptions and 196 compound effects; Big City 292, 586 and 470; Rush Hour 124, 250 and 375. About half the decal descriptions are constructed by the four other callers. With the making's, the makers', the decal description's and the compound description's comparisons on as well (Big City), 0 divergences in all. Four mistakes were put in on purpose on Big City, and each was reported in every construction of its kind: the speed 4, the first vector filled with 1, the compound effect's description index 0, and the decal's second block cleared four bytes on.

**A compound effect's records: the assign and the growth.** A record's assign (0x0058DBA0, (from) on the record) adds one to the record's count of changes and copies the thirteen floats and the byte +1. It takes over the effect (the new one held, slot 0x1C; the old one let go, slot 0x20) unless it is the same, and copies the part (+0x3C) and +0x40. The vector's growth (0x005905C0, (at, record, tag, count, atEnd) on the vector at +0x114) is called when the vector has no room. It allocates storage for the size plus the larger of the size and the count (0x0090CF54) and copy-constructs the records before `at` into it, then `count` copies of `record`. Unless `atEnd`, it copies the records from `at` on (0x0058EF60). It then ends the old records and frees their storage (0x00590040: what each record holds let go, slot 0x20, then the storage freed, 0x0090CF63).

`renderer.effects.records` replaces the assign, the growth and the destroy, written by hand in `src/core/effects_records.h`. The range copy stays the game's, because no test city inserts short of the end (the start always appends), so a translation of it would never run. The assign and the destroy hold or let go of effects. Their comparison is the small holder's: the game's first, with the holds and the endings noted and made through copies of the effects' tables of functions, then ours on the bytes put back with them only noted. The destroy's free is only noted too; the game's is taken as the free of the storage it had. The growth allocates and frees, so its comparison is the noted kind. The game's runs first, noting the allocation (in the pool allocator under the STL allocator), each copy constructed, the range copy and the destroy. Ours is then replayed on the vector put back, each call answered, and the calls and the vector are compared. The detours of the assign, the growth, the copy constructor and the pool allocator are shared with the start's and the makers' comparisons. On the three test cities with the camera workout, clouds and water effects on (2026-10-14), 0 divergences: Making Money 416 growths, 191 destroys and 48 assigns; Big City 818 and 454, with no assign; Rush Hour 755, 356 and 48. With the making's and the makers' comparisons on as well (Big City), 0 divergences in all. Mistakes were put in on purpose. The growth's room taken as the size plus the count was reported (98 of 817 growths), and so was the destroy freeing the vector's end (374 of 463). The assign's count changed by two and its test turned round were each reported in all 48 assigns on Rush Hour; on Big City they could not be, since it has none. (These were made while a translation of the range copy was still in the piece; a mistake put into it went unseen, the range copy never running, and the translation was taken out.)

**The look-up by IDs and two of the manager's starts.** Besides the name map, the manager keeps an ID map at +0xD00: a hash multimap of pairs of IDs to compound descriptions, with the same buckets as the name map. A node holds the next node (+0), the two IDs (+4, +8) and the index (+0xC), and a pair's bucket is the two IDs exclusive-ored, modulo the bucket count. Its equal range (0x0058EE80) and the look-up by IDs (0x0058F2E0) work as the name's do: of the descriptions of that pair, the look-up answers the index of the one with the largest word +4 above 0 and not above the manager's word at +0xB90, or -1. The start by IDs (0x00593AE0, (group, instance, out), a slot of the manager's interface with no direct callers) answers false without a place for the effect. It looks the pair up, and without a description it asks the ID map for a node of the pair (0x0058EF90), answering false when there is none. Otherwise it makes a compound effect (the maker above), with the IDs at +0x18 and +0x1C and the index at +0x20. One with a description is given it, and the waiting ones are started. The caller gets the compound effect, held once more (its slot 4). The restart (0x00593B90, called from four places further on) runs only when the manager's byte +0x1AD is set: it starts every compound effect in use again (the start above, with `first` clear), then the waiting ones.

`renderer.effects.lookup` now also replaces the look-up by IDs and its equal range, and `renderer.effects.starts` replaces the start by IDs and the restart, all written by hand in `src/core/effects_starts.h`. The ID map's node look-up (0x0058EF90) stays the game's; no test city calls it. The look-ups only read, so compare mode runs the game's and then ours. The starts make and start compound effects, so their comparison is the noted kind. The game's runs first, noting the look-ups, the maker, the description, the starts and the new compound effect's hold (through a copy of its table of functions). Ours is then replayed with each call answered, and the calls, the answer, the place the caller gets the compound effect and the three words written into it are compared. The restart's replay goes through a copy of the indices in use as they were, because the game's queued start at its end adds to them. A first run compared against the list as the game left it, and showed 13 divergences that were the comparison's. On the three test cities with the camera workout, clouds and water effects on (2026-10-14), 0 divergences: Making Money 59 starts by IDs, 82 restarts and 59 look-ups by IDs; Big City 175, 82 and 175; Rush Hour 82, 83 and 82. With the making's, the makers' and the start's comparisons on as well (Big City), 0 divergences in all. Four mistakes put in on purpose on Big City were each reported: the instance written as the group (all 175 starts by IDs), the restart's starts with `first` set (81 of 82), the restart's byte read one below (1 of 82), and the bucket of a pair taken as the sum of the IDs (64 of 175 look-ups by IDs and as many equal ranges).

**The start by name and the change of zoom.** The start by name (0x005939B0, (name, out), slot 0x1C of the manager's interface 0x00A9F264 with no direct callers) answers false without a name, with an empty one or without a place for the effect. It copies the name into the manager's string at +0x1A0 (0x0044D3A0) and lowers it there (0x00586AC0, a transform through the C runtime's `tolower`), then looks it up by name (above). Without a description it makes a string from the lowered name (0x00408000), looks for it in the manager's hash set of names at +0x98 (0x0051C9F0) and frees it, and answers false when it is not there either. Otherwise it makes a compound effect (the maker above), gives it the lowered name at +0xC (0x00408C40) and the index at +0x20, and one with a description is given it and the waiting ones are started. The caller gets the compound effect, held once more. The string helpers are all shared with code outside the effects (the transform is also called from 0x007DE28A on, the set's find from 0x0051CF76 on, the string functions from all over the game), so they stay the game's and ours calls them. 0x005939B0 is also where the UI Modernization mod puts an entry detour (`docs/patch-census.md`); with ours on, that mod's hook and ours would sit on the same entry, so the replacement is for the unmodified game, like the rest of this section. The change of zoom (0x00593BE0, (zoom), called only from the manager's message handler at 0x0059450A when the view's change 0xA6B79602 or 0xA6B79620 brings a new zoom) sets +0xC80 to the zoom, +0xC88 to the old +0xC84, and +0xC84 to the zoom less +0xC8C plus 1, at least 1. +0xCD0 is the table of floats at +0xCB4 read at the zoom times 0.25 times the table's last index, in a straight line between the two entries around it (the game takes the whole part by rounding x - 0.5 with 1.5 times 2 to the 23rd, then moves up one when x is one more; the in-between path never runs in the test cities, where x is always whole). +0xCD4 is the float at +0x1D4 of the object at [0x00B43CC4]+0x24 times the camera view's +0x150. Then every compound effect in use is started again (the restart above).

`renderer.effects.starts` now also replaces the start by name and the change of zoom, written by hand in `src/core/effects_starts.h`. The start by name is compared as the start by IDs is, noting the look-up by name as well. The lowered name each look-up sees and the name the new compound effect is given are compared too: the manager's string and that name are emptied before ours is replayed, and the game's are put back after. The manager's string itself is not compared after the run, because the game's queued start at the end runs other starts by name that use it, and in the replay that start is only noted. A first run compared it, and showed 1,318 divergences on Big City that were the comparison's. The change of zoom's comparison runs the game's with the restart noted, then ours on the five words put back, and compares the words and the calls. The restart under it is still compared on its own, with the change of zoom's notes set aside meanwhile; the first run without that compared only 1 restart instead of 82. On the three test cities with the camera workout, clouds and water effects on (2026-10-16), 0 divergences: Making Money 1,001 starts by name and 81 changes of zoom; Big City 8,676 and 81; Rush Hour 1,341 and 82; the starts by IDs, the restarts and the look-ups as before (59, 82 and 59 on Making Money, 175, 82 and 175 on Big City, 82, 83 and 82 on Rush Hour). Mistakes put in on purpose on Big City: the name not given to the new compound effect was reported in all 8,434 starts by name, the description taken one entry on in all 8,018, the old +0xC84 written one higher in all 81 changes of zoom, and +0xCD4 read from +0x1D0 of that object in all 81. Rounding the whole part to the nearest instead of down went unseen: in the test cities x is always whole, so the two agree.

`renderer.effects.store` replaces the store the effects file's compound descriptions are read into (the 0x38-byte objects of `SC4EffectCompound::Read`), written by hand in `src/core/effects_store.h`: an object's copy (0x0058A8C0) with the copies of its two lists (0x0058A330, 0x0058A3B0), the lists' ends (0x0058A630, 0x0058A420), a range copied and ended (0x0058B2D0, 0x0058BE50), the store's growth (0x0058C270), its insert (0x0058C8E0) and its two resizes (0x0058D070, and 0x0058D4D0, which the reader calls with an empty object; the game leaves the object's +0x24 and +0x28 as its stack held them and ours clears them, the reader filling both after the resize). The in-place moves of an insert with room (0x0058B300, 0x0058BA40, 0x0058B920) stay the game's; no test city inserts with room. The erase's copy down (0x0058B9A0) and the copies of the objects' records (0x005888A0, 0x005894C0) are `renderer.effects.tables`', whose detours the store's comparison shares. All eleven allocate or free, so the comparison is the noted kind, nested: the game's runs with each call it makes noted (the allocations in the pool allocator, the frees at 0x0090CF63, the game's helpers and these functions, each of which is itself compared where it is called), then ours is replayed with each call answered by the game's result and by the bytes the call left in its object, and the calls and those bytes are compared. A pointer into the stack is noted as such, and the object the resize is given by its bytes. The three ends read memory that the game's run frees, so for them ours runs first with its frees only noted. On the three test cities with the camera workout, clouds and water effects on (2026-10-16), 0 divergences, the same counts on each (the effects file is the same): 1 resize of the store to 1,154 objects, 1 growth, 1,154 object copies with their lists, 1,139 and 70 ends of the lists, 3 range ends and 1 range copy. Mistakes put in on purpose on Big City: two of the three plain words copied was reported in all 1,154 copies, the empty object's +4 cleared in the 1 resize, and the order of the two lists' ends turned round in 1 of the 3 range ends. The growth's room taken as the size plus the count went unseen: the store's one growth starts empty, where the two agree.

`renderer.effects.pointers` replaces the effect descriptions' lists of held pointers, the vectors the effects file reader resizes in place with a null value (0x005A46E0, 0x005A4820, 0x005A4940) and then fills, written by hand in `src/core/effects_pointers.h`. There are three kinds, the same code but for how an element is held and let go: objects whose counted base is at +4 (slots 0x10 and 0x14 of their tables, 0x005BE3E0 and 0x005BE3F0 in all six classes the reader makes), effect descriptions (`SC4Effect::Hold` 0x005AF940 and `SC4EffectDescription::Release` 0x005B1C80) and counted objects (slots 8 and 4, the counted base's own AddRef 0x00968B60 and Release 0x0090D96B; the 0x40-byte objects 0x005836A0 makes). For each kind the copy into raw storage (0x00586B70, 0x00586BF0, 0x00586C70), the fill (0x00586BB0, 0x00586C30, 0x00586CB0), the end (0x00587810, 0x00587850, 0x005877D0) and the growth (0x005880C0, 0x00588190, 0x00588260), and for the last two kinds the assignment, the erase's copy down (0x00586D50, 0x00586DC0): fourteen functions. The first kind's assignment (0x00586B00), the three copies back (0x00586CF0, 0x00586E30, 0x00586E90) and the manager's three in-place inserts (0x00587A00, 0x00587BA0, 0x00587D40) stay the game's; no test city reaches them. The comparison is the store's and shares its engine (nested and noted, the ends ours first). A hold or let-go is noted at the function the game reaches it by: the descriptions' two in their own detours, the other kinds' in detours, in compare mode only, on the four shared functions their tables' slots hold. Ours notes those by the function the kind's slot holds without reading the object, as the game's run may have let go of it last; a class whose slot held another function would show as a divergence. On the three test cities with the camera workout, clouds and water effects on (2026-10-19), 0 divergences, the same counts on each: 16 copies (12, 2 and 2 by kind), 8 fills (6, 1, 1), 8 growths (6, 1, 1), 2 assignments and 11 ends (8, 1, 2). All of them move null pointers: the reader resizes each list once, from empty, with a null value and fills the places afterwards with its own code, every end is the growth's end of the empty old storage, and both assignments have nothing to move. So no test city holds or lets go of anything through these fourteen, and the noting of holds and let-goes is untried. Mistakes put in on purpose on Big City: the growth's end of storage one place short was reported in all 8 growths. Four went unseen for that reason: the copy holding nothing, the end letting go of only the first or of none, and the assignment letting go before it holds. So did the growth's room taken as the size plus the count, since each growth starts empty.

`renderer.effects.vectors` replaces the effect descriptions' vectors of records and texts that the effects file reader (0x005A4E70, 0x005A51E0) fills and resizes, written by hand in `src/core/effects_vectors.h`. There are five kinds of element: decal descriptions (0x64 bytes; four lists of floats and a list of 12-byte items inside), records of 0x28 bytes (two lists of floats) and of 0x1C bytes (a list of 12-byte items and one of floats), and text records of 0x14 bytes (a string, two values) and of 0x10 bytes (a value, a string). Thirty functions: the decal description's copy (0x00587890); the range copies into raw storage of all five (0x00588330, 0x005883C0, 0x00588480, 0x00588B10, 0x00588BC0); the fills of the last four (0x00587960, 0x005879C0, 0x00588B70, 0x00588C10); the erase's copies down of the 0x28-byte records and the texts (0x00588560, 0x0058A4C0, 0x0058A540); the range ends of the first three (0x00588760, 0x00587EE0, 0x005887D0) and the end of a list of 0x10-byte texts (0x0058A880); the five growths (0x00589260, 0x00588C60, 0x00589380, 0x0058A770, 0x0058AF00); the inserts of the first three (0x0058A020, 0x00589100, 0x0058A170); the decal descriptions' resize (0x0058ADC0); the texts' erase (0x0058AEA0, 0x0058AFF0); the manager's emptying of its two texts' lists (0x0058BEF0, the end of 0x0058F690); and the end of a range of 0x20-byte name tables (0x0058B330: the objects in each table's list let go through slot 0x14, its hash table cleared by 0x0041A920). The copies and assignments of a list of floats, of 12-byte items and of a string stay the game's (0x00464760, 0x0050BCB0, 0x00464430, 0x00408420, 0x00408A70), as do the end of a list of 0x14-byte texts (0x005DB590), the 0x28-byte record's own copy (0x00587920) and the decal description's end (0x00583130), which only the inserts with room call, the in-place helpers of those inserts (0x00588360, 0x00588390, 0x00588420, 0x005884D0, 0x00588630, 0x00588680, 0x00588700) and the decal descriptions' copy down (0x00588510), which only the resize's shortening calls. The comparison is the store's and shares its engine (nested and noted, the ends ours first); the game's helpers are noted with the bytes they leave at their object, and the let-go of an object in a name table is noted at 0x005BE3F0, the detour the lists of held pointers use. The string copy's, the free's and that let-go's detours are shared between the groups. On the three test cities with the camera workout, clouds and water effects on (2026-10-19), 0 divergences, the same counts on each: 310 decal description copies, 9 range copies (1, 2, 2, 2, 2 by kind), 4 fills, 3 copies down, 9 ends (3, 2, 2 by kind, 2 of the 0x10-byte texts' lists), 5 growths, 3 inserts, 1 resize, 2 erases, 1 emptying of the manager's texts and 2 ends of name tables. Every insert and the resize reach their growth (each list is filled once, from empty), so the paths with room and the resize's shortening are untried. Mistakes put in on purpose on Big City: the decal description's byte +9 left uncopied was reported in all 310 copies, the 0x28-byte record's two lists copied the other way round and the 0x1C-byte record's value left out each in the 1 fill, the decal descriptions' lists freed in the other order in 1 of the 3 ends, the growth's end of storage one element short in all 5 growths, the manager's byte +0xD0 set in the 1 emptying, the name table's hash table not cleared in both ends, and the resize inserting one too many in the 1 resize: 8 of 16. Eight went unseen, all for what the test cities give these functions: the decal description's last value, the texts' values and the 0x28-byte records' last value in the copy down are 0 where they are copied (the reader fills the lists from empty default values and writes their contents with its own code afterwards), each growth starts empty (the room taken as the size plus the count), none grows by exactly one (the one copy made by the fill), the erased texts hold empty strings (the 0x14-byte texts' strings freed at the wrong place) and no insert has room (the insert growing when the room is just enough).

`renderer.effects.tables` replaces the effects tables, the 0x118-byte object (vtable 0x00A9F040) the effects file reader fills at its +4 and the manager holds, with the parts they are built of and three of the manager's own small functions, written by hand in `src/core/effects_tables.h`. The tables hold six hashes (the `SC4Uint32HashSet` layout), each followed by a list (the compound descriptions, held descriptions, counted objects, decal descriptions, 0x28- and 0x1C-byte records), 13 name tables, the zoom distances, the two texts' lists and a string. Sixteen functions: the tables' constructor (0x0058C580), end (0x0058C6E0) and take (0x0058D0E0: when the other's compound descriptions are empty the two swap their parts and the other gets a copy of the zoom distances; else the game's merge, 0x00587230 or 0x0058CA50, which stays the game's); the list of name tables made with empty ones (0x0058C1C0) and its fill (0x0058C0F0, the objects held through slot 0x10); a name hash's copy (0x00589BD0) and its insert after an equal name (0x00589E80); the zoom distances (0x00587F30); a compound description's 0x6C-byte record copy (0x005888A0) and range copy (0x00589490), its 0x18-byte record made (0x00588D90) and range copy (0x005894C0), and the compound descriptions' copy down (0x0058B9A0); the manager's AddRef (0x0058D840), QueryInterface (0x0058D9E0) and render states (0x0058D870). The bucket function (0x0051C9B0) and the render state's setter (0x007D2B50) stay the game's and, as they only read or write the state they are given, ours calls them in the replay too; the hash's constructor, a pointer vector's reserve and fill, the float append and the record lists' assignments (0x00829BC0, 0x0045D4F0, 0x004FBA20, 0x00676CC0, 0x0058B050, 0x0058B180) stay the game's and are noted. The comparison is the store's and shares its engine (nested and noted, the end ours first). Besides the bytes a function writes in place it can name memory elsewhere: what was there before is put back before ours runs (the other tables of a take, an insert's bucket and chain links), and storage the game's run allocated and ours fills (a hash's nodes and strings, a name table's list, the tables' string) is cleared first; both are compared. The functions of other groups the end calls are noted by those groups' detours, or, while a group is off, by detours of the tables' own; the store's and the vectors' helpers and the hold at 0x005BE3E0 share their detours. The QueryInterface the manager hands to the object at +0xD98 (0x0A5EAA1A, which the game jumps to) is noted as that call. On the three test cities with the camera workout, clouds and water effects on (2026-10-19), 0 divergences, the same counts on each but for the manager's: 2 constructions, 1 end, 1 take, 2 lists of name tables and their 2 fills, 26 hash copies, 1,154 inserts, 3 zoom distances, 3,420 record copies, 1,137 and 1,154 range copies, 10 records made, 1 copy down, 1 render states, 5 own QueryInterfaces, and the AddRefs and handed QueryInterfaces (32,984 and 9.5 million on Making Money, 178,223 and 12.6 million on Big City, 16,528 and 14 million on Rush Hour). Mistakes put in on purpose on Big City, 13 of 19 reported: the record's last dword, the 0x18-byte record's +0x14, an equal name's node put at the bucket's head, the insert's count and value, the first zoom distance, the tables' byte +0x108 and string end byte, a hash left out of the end, a dword left out of the take's swap, the AddRef answering the count before, a render state's value and the QueryInterface not holding the manager. Six went unseen, for what the test cities give these functions: the record's byte +0x15 is 0, every hash copied is empty (its count and a chain's values), the one copy down is of an empty range, every name table's list is empty (its end), and no one asks for 0xC97CF5CE (its interface's offset).

`renderer.effects.hashes` replaces the effects manager's hash tables and small objects, written by hand in `src/core/effects_hashes.h`, and its lists of held effects, a fourth kind of the lists of held pointers' code (`src/core/effects_pointers.h`) with this group's mode. Sixteen functions: a dword hash's look-up (0x0058EFE0) and end (0x0058F3A0, the clear 0x008EBA60 inlined; in ours' replay noted as that one call when `renderer.effects.tail`'s comparison detours it, as the game's is then); a hash of key pairs' insert unless the pair is there (0x0058F230) and its growth (0x0058F3C0); the growth of a hash of names (0x00587010, a name's hash h * 5 plus each character); a growth takes the first of 28 primes at 0x00A829B0 not below the count (0x0079FA90 and the bucket vector's constructor 0x0051C950 inlined) and moves each old chain's nodes to the head of their new bucket; the clear of a hash of lists (0x00584660) and of a list of events (0x00584210, each node's object let go through its slot 8); the 0x40-byte counted object's constructor (0x005836A0), destructor (0x00583720) and deleting destructor (0x00583700); the character transform (0x00586AC0); and the manager's lists of held effects (+0xBC4 to +0xC54): copy (0x0058DFE0), fill (0x0058DFA0), assignment (0x0058F020), end (0x0058F940) and growth (0x0058FF70), held and let go through slots 0x1C and 0x20. Two classes are in those lists: the effects of table 0x00AA0EA0, whose slots hand the call to their counted base at +4 (0x005A8F80, 0x005A8F90), and the decals of 0x00A9EE78 (the shared AddRef 0x005F47F0 and the decal's Release 0x005833A0); in compare mode all four are detoured and each is noted as the hold or let-go ours notes. The comparison is the store's and shares its engine (nested and noted, the ends and clears ours first, with the bytes they write compared too). A growth's game's run frees the old buckets whose bytes and links are put back for ours, and an end's frees what ours then reads, so these comparisons keep the frees (0x0090CF63) and deletes (0x005E5620, detoured in compare mode) of the game's run until they are done. The look-up, the dword hash's end, the clears, the names' growth and the transform are the whole game's, so the group compares only on the thread it was installed on. On the three test cities with the camera workout, clouds and water effects on (2026-10-19), 0 divergences: 67 pair inserts and 67 pair growths, 2,980 name growths, 15 clears of events, 3 constructions, destructions and deletions of the counted object (4 constructions), 5 assignments of held effects on each; and 1.3, 5.8 and 1.1 million look-ups, 9,680, 7,069 and 12,425 transforms, 1,007, 7,001 and 1,425 clears of a hash of lists, 61, 53 and 69 ends of a dword hash, 25, 28 and 26 copies, ends and growths of held effects on Making Money, Big City and Rush Hour. No test city fills more than one held effect at once (0x0058DFA0). Mistakes put in on purpose on Big City, 17 of 24 reported: the look-up's key, a moved node's link, the name hash by 7, the new buckets' end of storage, the old buckets not freed, the insert's count and value, the end's count and storage, a node's list and the buckets in the clear, the counted object's float, string room and first byte, the destructor's table, the deleting destructor's flag and the transform. Seven went unseen, for what the test cities give these functions: no count passed to a growth is a prime itself (the bound) or past the last prime, the pair hash never grows with nodes in it (its hash), no pair is inserted twice, every list of events cleared is empty (the let-go and the sentinel's back link) and the destroyed counted objects hold nothing at +0x34.

`renderer.effects.heightmap` replaces the particles' height map, the 0x88-byte object (vtable 0x00A9F008) the effects manager makes at its +0xD98 (and is itself built on at its +0xC), which a particle meeting the ground asks for the height and the slope under it (0x005B8DC0, through the manager's interface 0x0A5EAA1A), written by hand in `src/core/effects_heightmap.h`. It holds the terrain (+0xC, taken from 0x00B43CF8 at its init), the terrain's sea level, cell counts and cell size, and a vector of layers of one float per vertex of the terrain's grid (one layer of zeros at the init). Twelve functions: the query (0x00585AD0: 0x0A5EAA1A the map, held; any other id the counted base's, 0x009457C6); a vertex's height (0x00586030, slot 7: the terrain's, kept as a float, plus the first layer's value; under the sea the higher of the terrain and the sea level plus the value less 50, rounded to a float only on that side); the height at a place (0x00585B00, slot 6: from the four vertices around it, the weight along z the fraction in x and the other way round, as the game has it, the two values at x kept as floats and the two at x + 1 not); the ground's normal (0x005860A0, slot 8: slopes from sixteen vertex heights around the place, the indices kept on the grid, scaled by one over the length); the three changes of a layer, each over count + 1 rings from a cell rectangle out, cut to the grid (0x0048ED90, the game's) and one wider each time, setting +0x48 (the raise 0x005863D0, amount / (count + 1) added in each ring; the lift 0x005864F0, a value below the height raised to it, the height times 1 - 1 / (count + 1) for each ring out; the clear 0x00586640, one ring of zeros); the constructor (0x00586890), the init (0x005869C0), the shutdown (0x00586990) and the deleting destructor (0x00586940) with the thunk in the counted base's table (0x005868F0). Places are floored as the game does it, v - 0.5 rounded through 1.5 x 2^23 to a float and one more when that is v; the cell size is truncated as _ftol2 does; the x87 runs at 53 bits, so every intermediate is a double, rounded to float where the game stores one, and the heights are answered as doubles. The line list at +0x4C (0x008012F0, 0x008011F0), the vector helpers (0x00464340, 0x00464130, 0x004643B0, 0x004638B0) and the counted base's functions stay the game's, and the map's own lines (their making 0x00585C20 and draw 0x00586720), the clear of its values (0x00586700) and its plain destructor (0x00586900), none of which the test cities reach, are `renderer.effects.heightmap_rest` (below). The comparison runs both on what each may change: the query, the two heights and the normal only read, so the game's and then ours, the answers compared to the bit (the query's with the count it raised at +8, put back after ours); a change of a layer has the layer and +0x48 put back after the game's, then ours, and the game's left; the constructor runs ours on a copy of the memory first; the init, the shutdown and the destructor run ours first on a copy whose layers are its own storage and whose terrain is held once more, then the game's, and compare the bytes with the layers' pointers by what they hold (null alike, the same used and room, after an init the same values). The destructor's game's run is made without the delete flag and the delete made after it, ours' delete only noted; the thunk is compared by the map and flags the destructor's detour gets. Inside another group's noted run only the game's runs. On the three test cities with the camera workout, clouds and water effects on (2026-10-20), 0 divergences, all twelve reached: 2 constructions, 1 init, shutdown, destruction and thunk on each; 1.6, 2.2 and 1.3 million vertex heights, 173,333, 130,119 and 162,416 heights, 8,407, 49,889 and 1,992 normals, 7.1, 10.9 and 13.1 million queries, 7,101, 72,222 and 2,187 lifts, 0, 1,183 and 48 raises, 1, 16 and 17 clears on Making Money, Big City and Rush Hour. Both sides of the vertex height under the sea and the clamps at both edges of the grid are reached; not reached in any of them: a query for another id, an init with layers already there or a terrain already held, a shutdown with no terrain, the destructor without the delete flag, a change of any layer but the first, and a ring off the grid. Mistakes put in on purpose on Big City, 33 of 38 reported: the query's id and hold, the floor's one more, a vertex's row, the sea's 50, the higher of the two under the sea and its float, the sum's rounding above the sea, the height's first float and its weights, the normal's scale, its x and z, its length, its upper index and a slope's weight, a ring's growth, the layer's number, the changed byte, the raise's step and add, the lift's factor and compare, the clear's value, the constructor's base table and changed byte, the destructor's delete flag and base, the shutdown's terrain and erase, and the init's sea level, inverse, bounds and values. Five went unseen: the height's z kept as a double gives the same answers here, Big City never clamps an index at the grid's low edge (Making Money and Rush Hour do), the constructor's memory is cleared already (determinism.zero_alloc), the destructor's base table is written again by the base's destructor, and the cell size is a whole 16.

**The height map's rest.** The map draws its own lines, a debug view: the effects manager's draw calls 0x00586720 (ret 4, with the render context; named `SC4EffectsManager::BeforeDraw` before) on the map when the byte at the interface's +0xD98 is set. Without layers it does nothing. With +0x48 set (a layer changed) the line list at +0x4C is made anew first (0x00585C20, below). Then +0x48 is cleared, a render state made on two cleared words (0x007D2B50 with 1, 1, 1, 1, 1, 0, 0, 3, 4, 5, 4, 0.9, 1, -1), set on the context (0x007D4530), and the line list, brought up to date (0x00801140, which answers the list itself), drawn as the context's mesh (0x007D4A80); the manager's draw does not look at the answer. 0x00586700 (no arguments) sets every value of the first layer to zero without looking whether there is a layer; the manager calls it at 0x00592C0C. 0x00586900 is the plain destructor, the deleting one's work without the delete; the manager's destructor ends in a jump to it for the map it is built on.

`renderer.effects.heightmap_rest` replaces the three, written by hand in `src/core/effects_heightmap.h` (2026-10-25, `DrawHeightMapLines`, `ClearHeightMapValues`, `EndHeightMap`; off by default). The lines' draw has one detour, which the manager's draw's comparison notes it through and this group's mode runs otherwise. Compare mode runs ours and then the game's on the same map and context, both drawing, with the calls the draw is made of noted at their entry by detours of compare mode (the lines made anew, the render state's two words and fourteen values, the line list asked for its mesh) and passed on, +0x48 put back between the two; the notes and the map's bytes before the line list must be the same. The context's own two calls are not noted (other switches detour them). The clear and the destructor run ours on a copy whose layers are its own and the game's on the map, compared as the height map's destructor; the destructor's answer (the base's end's) is not compared. No test city reaches any of the three. With `OPENSC4_HEIGHTMAP_FORCE=1` (compare mode only) the manager's draw runs them first on the manager's map when it has layers: the lines each frame (every fourth with +0x48 set, so that they are made anew) and on a copy without layers, and every eighth frame the clear and the destructor, each on two copies whose layers are their own (the destructor's with line lists made anew), the game's on one and ours on the other; they are recorded as `effects.heightmap_rest.*.forced`. 2026-10-25, three test cities with the camera workout, clouds and water effects on, sound off, forcing on: 0 divergences in 316, 1,448 and 1,392 lines' draws and 20, 91 and 87 clears and destructors (Big City, Making Money, Rush Hour; the forcing slows the frames: the making of the lines asks the map's height and normal at every vertex it marks); a side run on Big City with every `renderer.effects.*` switch ours, the manager's draw, the height map and this group compared and forcing on: 0 divergences (52 draws of the manager, 104 lines' draws, 7 clears and destructors, 33 million vertex heights). Mistakes put in on purpose (Big City, forced), 7 of 11 reported: the check for layers left out, +0x48 not cleared, the lines made when +0x48 is clear, the state's 0.9 as 0.5, its 3 as 2, the mesh not asked for, the clear started at the second value. Unseen: the clear stopping one short (the last vertex's value is zero there), and three in the destructor: the terrain not let go (its count is not in the map's bytes), the tables not put back (the copies have them already), the line list not ended (a list made anew holds nothing). With every `renderer.effects.*` switch ours and forcing off: parity 12 of 12 for 7 days, screenshots 20 of 20 in the three cities against two runs of main (Big City's two runs of main matched each other in 19).

The lines' making (0x00585C20, no arguments) does nothing without layers. It empties the line list (0x00801660), sets its +0xC to (0.25, 0.5, 1) (0x00800F80), its colour to yellow (0x00800F30 with 0x00B0A23C; the list's +0x24 to +0x2C and the float at 0x00B0E358, 1, at +0x30) and its normal up (0x00800F10). Then for each vertex, rows (+0x2C) and columns (+0x28) read anew, whose first layer's value is not zero (a NaN is not): a box from the terrain's height (its slot 0x38, kept as a float) up to the map's (slot 7, the first layer), half as wide as the cell size (made a float) times 0.45 (the double at 0x00A9EFE8, kept as a float), yellow; red (0x00B0A230) when the map's height is below the terrain's or unordered, its top then mirrored above the terrain (twice the difference added, unrounded). Its corners are laid out (0x00800FA0: x alternating, then y, then z, the eight first filled with the float at 0x00B22C50) and added (0x008014D0, its six faces as lines). Then in blue (0x00B0A248) a flat box at the top, half as wide as the size times 0.1 (the double at 0x00A9EFE0, unrounded), whose four upper corners are moved by the map's normal there (slot 8) times the size, added the same way. The x87 keeps some values unrounded and stores others: the first box's low x and z come from the unrounded place, its high ones from the stored one; of the normal's moves, x is added unrounded to the corners 3 and 6 and as a float to 2 and 7, y always unrounded, z unrounded to 2 and as a float to the others.

Since 2026-10-25 the making is part of `renderer.effects.heightmap_rest` too (`MakeHeightMapLines`). Its detour notes it for the draw's comparison and runs it by the group's mode. Its comparison runs ours with the line list's calls (the clear, the +0xC, the colour, the normal, each box with its eight corners) only noted and not made, then the game's with the same calls noted at their entry by detours of compare mode and passed on; the notes must be the same. The reads (the terrain's and the map's heights, the map's normal) run in both. The forcing of the draw reaches it, and every eighth frame (`OPENSC4_HEIGHTMAP_FORCE=1`) it also runs on a copy of the map whose first layer's values are negated (the map's height below the terrain's) and whose cell size is 13 (products not exact as floats), with a line list of its own (`effects.heightmap_rest.make_lines.forced`). 2026-10-25, three test cities with the camera workout, clouds and water effects on, sound off, forcing on: 0 divergences in 290, 1,258 and 936 makings and 18, 79 and 58 on the copy (Big City, Making Money, Rush Hour). Mistakes put in on purpose (Big City, forced), 6 of 7 reported: the half-width's 0.45 as 0.1, the red branch left out (seen only on the copy), the normal's z added to corner 2 as a float (seen only on the copy), every vertex marked, the values' index by columns, the terrain asked with x and z swapped. Unseen: the first box's high x from the unrounded place, which is the same value for a whole cell size (the place is the column times the size, exact as a float). With every `renderer.effects.*` switch ours and forcing off: parity 12 of 12 for 7 days, screenshots 20 of 20 in the three cities against two runs of main.

`renderer.effects.decal_parts` replaces a decal's small functions (the class of table 0x00A9EE78, 0x124 bytes) and two of the manager's, written by hand in `src/core/effects_decal.h`; the update was generated (`effects_decal_update.inc`, removed 2026-10-22) and is `renderer.effects.decal_update` since 2026-10-23 ("A decal's update" above); the counts below are from before. Eight functions: the parameter setter (0x00582F00, slot 5: id 3 the word to +0x108, 0x102 the second word to +0x10C, 0xC a rectangle giving the middle, width, depth over width and with the description's flag 8 the width; false for other ids); the update (0x00582600, slot 3: unless +0x110 is set, the place, scale and turn from two transforms, the turn the x87's own arctangent; with the description's flag 0x40 and +0x70 set, values for the object at +0x120 through its slots 0x38 and 0x34); Release (0x005833A0, slot 8: the count down, at zero the object at +0x120 and the texture let go, the description ended and the decal deleted); the description's end (0x00583130: its five lists' storage freed) and assignment (0x005833F0: the words and bytes copied, the four lists of floats assigned by 0x00464430 and the list of three floats by 0x0050C540, which stay the game's); the manager's fill of held pointers (0x0058DF60, each held through its slot 1) and the AddRef thunks of its interfaces at +4 and +8 (0x0058FD60, 0x0058FD80). The decal's draw (0x00582090) is `renderer.effects.decal_draw` ("A decal's draw" above). Its reader (0x005837C0) is `renderer.effects.decal_read` (below). The manager's two Release thunks (0x0058FD70, 0x0058FD90), which may end the manager, stay the game's; so does the rest of the manager's tail (0x0058DE80 on), whose functions act through virtual calls on objects no comparison can note. The setter only writes the decal, so the game's runs, the decal is put back, ours runs, and the two are compared and the game's left. The assignment, the end and Release are the store's kind and share its engine: the assignment's game's run noted, its list assignments with the bytes they leave, and ours replayed on the description put back; the end and Release run ours first, then the game's noted, with its frees and deletes kept (a Release at zero with an object at +0x120, whose let-go has no detour, runs the game's only). These three addresses had detours of the decal's description, the vectors and the hash tables, which note them in their own comparisons; each now has one detour, by this group's mode and noted in a noted run whatever that mode, and the three groups were compared again with it (0 divergences). The fill runs the game's and then ours on storage of its own, comparing the pointers and giving ours' holds back (slot 2); a thunk compares the answer and the count at the manager's +0x1B0, put back after the game's. On the three test cities with the camera workout, clouds and water effects on (2026-10-20, sound off), 0 divergences, all eight reached: 71,659, 1,370,749 and 80,527 updates, 82,754, 101,974 and 93,402 parameters, 703, 8,960 and 797 Releases, 182, 7,570 and 198 assignments, 108, 295 and 125 ends, 36,187, 178,242 and 20,512 AddRefs through +8, and one or two AddRefs through +4 and fills, on Making Money, Big City and Rush Hour. Not reached in any of them (a probe on Big City and Rush Hour): the update's calls on the object at +0x120, the parameter 0xC and any other id, and a Release at zero before the city closes (about 300 come as it closes). Mistakes put in on purpose on Big City, 12 of 19 reported: the parameter 3's place and 0x102's word, a list left out of the end, the list of three floats assigned as floats, Release taking two, not deleting at zero and not ending the description, the fill's answer, a thunk's answer, and the update's turn, scale and place. Seven went unseen, for what the test cities give these functions: no parameter 0xC or other id (the rectangle's factor, the answer for another id), the bytes and words an assignment copies are already the same in the decal (+9, +0x60), the table Release puts back at zero is the one the decal has, the fill is compared by its pointers (its holds), and on Big City no update has a second transform (its branch). The compare runs, the workout runs and these were made with the sound off (OPENSC4_MUTE=1); the screenshots with it on, as the two runs of the unmodified game were (a first run after the merge gave 19 and 20, the zoom-1 rotation-1 smoke shot at 1.23%; the second 20 and 20).

`renderer.effects.decal_read` replaces the reader at 0x005837C0 (cdecl, (stream, object)), written by hand in `src/core/effects_decal.h` (2026-10-24). What it reads is not a decal but the 0x40-byte counted object (0x005836A0) an effect description's list at +0x5C holds; the effects file reader (0x005A51E0, 0x005A5D97) makes one for each, constructs it and has it read. Every value comes through the stream's own functions: a word of seven bits to +8 (the stream's slot 0x24, read into the stream argument's own slot, so a read that wrote nothing would leave the stream's pointer there), the string at +0xC (0x005811F0), a word to +0x30, the list of IDs at +0x34 (0x005DB350), then the six floats +0x18 to +0x2C (slot 0x30). It answers what the last read answers. The two helpers stay as they are (0x005DB350 is the save streams' when theirs is on). The comparison is the compound descriptions' readers': the game's reads with the stream's reads noted (the copy of its table that `renderer.effects.lists` makes), and ours reads the same into a copy of the object as it was, with an empty string of its own, and must make the same reads and answer the same. The two must then hold the same word, text, floats, word and IDs. On the three test cities with the camera workout (sound off), 0 divergences: 6 objects read at the start in each, also with every other `renderer.effects.*` switch ours on Big City. Each object makes 10 reads: every list of IDs is empty (only its count is read) and every word is below 0x80. Not reached: a list of IDs with any in it, and a read that writes nothing. Mistakes put in on purpose on Big City, 9 of 12 reported: the string, the word at +0x30, the IDs, the first float left out, the reads out of order (twice), a float or the word read through the wrong slot (twice), a float to the wrong place and the answer. Three went unseen, for what the effects file gives: the mask of 0xFF (no word reaches 0x80), the word's starting value (every read writes it) and +8 stored as a byte (its upper bytes are already 0). Parity 12 of 12 for 7 days with every `renderer.effects.*` switch ours; screenshots 20 of 20 on the three cities against two runs of the unmodified game (clouds off, sound on).

`renderer.effects.tail` replaces fifteen of the manager's small functions that act through other objects' tables or the game's helpers, written by hand in `src/core/effects_tail.h`: the script registry's register (0x0058DE80: the script's slots 0x54, 0x24 and 0xC, the object's unknown asked for through its slot 0 and let go) and unregister (0x0058DF10); an effect's follow-up start (0x0058E340: its +0x144 given to the renderer at 0x00B43DD0 and cleared, and when its record in the store has a follow-up name, the effect the manager's slot 0x1C starts by it given the effect's +0x6C, +0x34 and slot 0xC with 0, then let go); the place given to the compound effects in use with flag 1 (0x0058E4A0, their slot 0x20 with their +0x6C); the Release thunks of the manager's interfaces at +4 and +8 (0x0058FD70, 0x0058FD90), which jump to a shared stub that answers 1 (0x008CD450); and the lift of the particles' floor under an occupant: the check (0x0058E260, the occupant in EDI, an LTCG convention, handed on by a naked thunk: its type must not have 0x029244DB and must have one of 0x27812820 to 0x27812824, then a building lifts, and anything else by its type's property 0x8A5E5DB8), the lift (0x0058E720: the height map's first layer raised to the occupant's height over its cell rectangle one wider to the high side, in two rings, through the map's slot 0x28, and the highest top kept at +0xDA0) and its message handler (0x0058F140). Since 2026-10-21 also the manager's factory (0x005919B0: 0xDF4 bytes from operator new, then its constructor 0x005912B0; the allocation is noted where operator new gives it, at the pool allocator, under determinism.zero_alloc's own detour of operator new, and the constructor by a detour of this group's in compare mode) and the options' application (0x00593E10: values from the preferences at 0x00B43CC4 to +0xCA0, +0xCA4, +0xCD4, +0xC8C and the level +0xB90, a byte to the service at 0x00B43CA0, the object 0x09CD86F9 of the one the +0xDE0 object names given to every effect at +0xBE8 or taken away, a detail level and two values to the framework's 0x1AC0E11A object, the effects in use restarted when the level changed, and the two held effects at +0xD90 and +0xD94 stopped or started by name). The effect's SetObject (the holders' and the makers') and the restart of the effects in use (the starts') now note in the store's noted runs too, the restart keeping one detour whichever group makes it. Later the same day also the tables' interface's start (0x005900A0, slot 4: an empty function first, 0x00407990, which ours leaves out; the floats at +0xD4 assigned to +0xC20 and four words copied, the list read at the city view's zoom into +0xC3C with the 1.5 x 2^23 floor; each name's equal range in the name map at +4, 0x00586EF0, walked node by node, to the next bucket's first at a chain's end, its values put into the pair hash at +0xC6C after a growth to one more, 0x0058F3C0 and 0x0058F230; the messages at +0xFC numbered in the map at +0xC80, 0x004F8D30, and listened for through the message server's slot 0x14) and an occupant's going away (0x00590380: its cells cleared in the height map, the map's slot 0x2C, and every occupant of type 0x278128A0 around them, from the occupant manager's query at 0x00B43D0C, slot 0xA4, into a list from the node allocator at 0x00B207E4, lifting it again through 0x0058E720 and let go, the list ended by 0x00413FE0). The pair hash's growth and insert (the hash tables') and the name map's equal range (the look-up's) now keep one detour each that notes them in a noted run whatever those groups' modes, as the transform and the texts' clear do; an insert's note keeps the pair it is given and its out bytes, so ours keeps the pair hash's count itself (one more when the insert says it put the pair in, which in a replay the replay raises too). For the going away, the query's note keeps the two links of the list's sentinel (an out behind a pointer), the occupants the query put in the list are proxied for their let-go, and the list's end is noted in the game's run and kept until the comparison is done (ours reads the list again), then made; a comparison before the node allocator exists runs the game's only. Since 2026-10-20 also the check whether a name is a compound description's (0x005916C0, slot 5 of the manager's table: none for no name or an empty one; the name copied into the manager's string at +0x1A0 by 0x0044D3A0, lowered in place by the character transform with CRT::tolower, and looked up in the hash of names at +0x98 by 0x0051C9F0) and the tables' interface's stop (0x0058F690, slot 3: each message the manager listens for taken off the message server at 0x00B43CCC through its slot 0x18, the dword hashes at +0xC6C and +0xC80 cleared by 0x008EBA60, then the texts' clear 0x0058BEF0). Their helpers are noted by detours of this group's in compare mode (the string's assignment, with the 12 bytes it leaves; the hash's look-up; the dword hash's clear, with the 20 bytes it leaves), and the character transform and the texts' clear, which the hash tables and the vectors detour, now keep one detour each that notes them in a noted run whatever those groups' modes (the two groups were compared again with it, 0 divergences). These had stayed the game's because a comparison could not note calls through other objects' tables; this group adds a way. During a comparison the objects the function will call are proxied: each gets a copy of its table (the word before it and 64 slots) in which the slots that function uses go to a thunk, and is given its own table back at the end. In the game's noted run a thunk notes the call (the function the slot held, the object, the words passed, folded into one when there are more than three, the bytes behind a pointer passed in, such as the lift's rectangle) with the bytes it leaves at one or two out pointers, and makes it; in ours' replay it answers from the note and writes those bytes back; else it only makes the call. An object a noted call hands back (at an out pointer or in EAX: a queried unknown, the effect started, the occupant's type) is proxied too, as the kind the slot names. The engine is the store's (StCompared), so the calls and what the function writes (the effect's +0x144, the manager's +0xDA0) are compared as for the others. The two direct helpers the check calls, the query for a held interface (0x0048FF50) and a property's truth (0x005FD390), are noted by detours of this group's, in compare mode only. On the three test cities with the camera workout, clouds and water effects on (2026-10-20, sound off), 0 divergences, all nine reached: 21 registers, 12 unregisters, 40 places and one Release through +4 on each; 5,118, 17,012 and 14,931 follow-ups, 13,136, 88,144 and 8,142 checks (each lift's own among them), 12,029, 88,109 and 6,755 lifts, 10,949, 87,153 and 5,682 messages and 32,970, 178,242 and 21,482 Releases through +8 on Making Money, Big City and Rush Hour. The two added later, on the three cities (2026-10-20, sound off): 0 divergences in 8,862, 9,880 and 11,178 name checks and one stop on each. The start and the going away, on the three cities (2026-10-20, sound off): 0 divergences in one start and 12,322, 87,668 and 6,425 goings away on Making Money, Big City and Rush Hour; the hash tables and the look-up compared again with the shared detours, 0 divergences. The factory and the options, on the three cities (2026-10-21, sound off): one of each, 0 divergences; the holders, the makers and the starts compared again, 0 divergences. Mistakes put in on purpose in these two on Big City, 13 of 17 reported: the manager's size, and in the options four values' sources, the service's byte, the interface asked for and its hold, the detail's scale, a restart with the level the same, a held effect's name, the host's last call, the holder's end and the asked object's let-go (two of them first ended the run: with a call it could not answer the replay went on to a null object, which in a replay is now only noted). Four went unseen: memory is never short, the effects' setting is on, two of the preferences' words are the same, and so are the two held effects' settings. Mistakes put in on purpose in these two on Big City, 11 of 15 reported: the floats' source, a word left out of the copy, a name's hash by 7 (the run ends: the walk leaves the chain, as the game's would), the pair's second word (once an insert's note held the pair's 12 bytes and not only where they were), and in the going away the cleared rectangle, the query's corner and type, the lift again, the let-go, the list's end and the layer cleared (16 of the goings away on Big City reach the query). Four went unseen: the list read at the zoom holds one value (its scale and its fraction), the number written into the map's value is not looked at by the comparison, and the manager listens for no message. Mistakes put in on purpose in these two on Big City, 6 of 9 reported: the name a character short, not lowered, the wrong hash and the answer the other way, the second hash not cleared and the texts not cleared. Three went unseen: no empty name is asked about, and the manager listens for no message when it stops (the target and a message's word). With these on, Big City's screenshots matched the two runs of the unmodified game in 19 and 20 of 20 at first (the zoom-1 rotation-1 smoke shot at 1.26%), 20 and 20 again. Mistakes put in on purpose on Big City (sound off), 17 of 21 reported: the register's flags, word order, kept unknown and never asked object, the unregister's id and kept unknown, the place's flag, a Release thunk's answer, the check's excluded id, a left-out id, a building that does not lift and the property not looked at, and the lift's height, rectangle, highest top and rings and the message's answer. Four went unseen, for what the test cities give these functions: no effect's record has a follow-up name and no effect's +0x144 is set (the follow-up's clear, place and slot 0xC), and no compound effect in use has flag 1 (the place's word); so the follow-up start and the place's calls are compared only as never happening, and the kinds they would proxy (the effect started) are untried.

`renderer.effects.time` replaces the manager's time step and an effect's start actions, written by hand in `src/core/effects_time.h`. The time step (0x0058E580, once a tick): the step in ms times the speed of the service at 0x00B43D5C (its slot 0x80, answering on the x87 stack), truncated as _ftol2, added to the time at +0x1B8 and in seconds (times 0.000001) at +0x1BC; the cursor's screen place (the framework's object 0xA417445E/0x5A4, slot 0x84) made a ray into the world by the city view (0x007F9080); each compound effect in use that is running (slot 0x14) given the ray's point and direction (slot 0x2C with 9, the point and 2: it hands on 2 vectors, six floats from the point, so the two lie side by side as on the game's stack; with flag 0x20 at +0x28) and its part at +4 the step in seconds (slot 0x10); the cursor's holder let go (0x004177F0). The start actions (0x005908A0, from the compound effect's start): the effect's place on the ground (its point at +0x94 by the matrix at +0x38, as floats where the game stores them, times its scale and plus its offset), +0x144 cleared, then each action of its record in the store: a sound (the renderer's slot 0xA8 with the sound at +0x12C and, with flag 2, the place; the handle kept at +0x144), a shake at the place (0x00590540, the game's) or the manager's own shake (+0xD28 and +0xD34 assigned by 0x0050C540 and 0x00464430, its length, its inverse, +0xD44 and +0xD4C cleared), or a flash (0x0058FE70, the game's). The comparison is the tail's: the speed service (its slot answering a double kept with the note), the framework, the cursor object and the renderer are proxied; the city view's ray, the holder's let-go, the shake and the flash are noted by detours of this group's in compare mode, as are the compound effect's running flag (0x007D6680), point (0x005BEA50) and part's step (0x005BEA10), since proxying the effects themselves does not hold up (below). The step's effects can start, end and be made by copying others during the game's run, so a step is compared only when the compound effects in use are the same before and after the game's run; the others are counted, not compared. This batch also made the call recording sturdier. The copies of tables are now kept for good, one for each table and set of kinds, and an object the game's run made by copying a proxied one's bytes keeps working through its copy, which hands its calls on (a first version freed the copies and such an object then called itself until the stack ran out). Other threads reach the thunks too (the renderer is the sound's), so the proxies and copies are looked at and changed under a lock, never held across a call. In ours' replay a call on an object not holding its proxy's copy is never made, only noted and answered. On the three test cities with the camera workout, clouds and water effects on (2026-10-20, sound off), 0 divergences, both reached: 74,962, 57,199 and 80,314 steps compared (on Big City 128 to 255 not, the effects in use having changed in the game's run) and 4,019, 8,229 and 5,382 start actions on Making Money, Big City and Rush Hour; the tail's comparison was run again on the three with the changed recording, 0 divergences. Mistakes put in on purpose on Big City (sound off), 9 of 20 reported: the speed, the time, the seconds, the framework's class (the run ends: with no cursor ours has nothing to ask, as the game would), the running check, the point's flag and words, the part's step and the holder kept. Eleven went unseen, for what the test cities give these functions: the cursor's x and z are the same in the automatic runs (the ray's two words swapped), and no effect started in them has a start action, so the place, the cleared handle, the sound, its place, the shake's three values and the flash are compared only as never happening. The point's call is noted by its pointer, not the floats behind it, so the comparison also missed that ours first kept the point and the direction apart: the effects were handed three floats of stack after the point as the direction, and the game's pull on an effect's particles (0x005B6C40) then read a NaN and crashed at 0x005B6F27 at the start of some runs (Making Money's test save at day 1095, about a third of runs made one at a time, none with several games running). Fixed 2026-10-23: 0 crashes in 40 runs since, parity 12 of 12 for 7 days with every `renderer.effects.*` switch ours.

With all sixty-seven replacements of this section on, the camera workout ran to its end on the three cities (sound off), and Big City's screenshots with clouds off (sound on, as the two runs of the unmodified game) match those two runs in 20 and 20 of 20 (2026-10-25, with the flash and the shake; 2026-10-20, with the time step and start actions; with sixty-four, 2026-10-20, with the manager's calls through other tables; with sixty-three, 2026-10-20, with a decal's small functions; with sixty-two, 2026-10-20, with the height map; with sixty-one, 2026-10-19, with the hash tables; with sixty, 2026-10-19, with the effects tables; with fifty-nine, 2026-10-19, with the vectors of records and texts; a first run gave 19 and 20, the zoom-1 rotation-3 smoke shot at 1.32% against one of them; with fifty-eight, 2026-10-19, with the lists of held pointers; with fifty-seven, 2026-10-16, with the store; before it, with fifty-six, also 20 and 20, with the start by name and the change of zoom; a first run gave 19 and 20, the zoom-1 rotation-1 shot at 1.38% against one of them, all of it industrial smoke, with the two runs of the unmodified game 0.75% apart in that shot). The counts as the section grew: with fifty-six before them 20 and 20 (2026-10-14), with fifty-five and fifty-four replacements 20 and 20 (a first run with fifty-four gave 19 and 19, the two zoom-1 smoke shots at 1.56% and 1.21%), with fifty-three, fifty-two and fifty 20 and 20 (a first run with fifty gave 18 and 20, two zoom-1 shots at 1.21% and 1.36%, the smoke below), with forty-nine 19 and 20, with forty-eight 20 and 20, with forty-seven 19 and 20.

**The smoke shot.** One of the twenty Big City screenshots, zoom 1 rotation 3, is mostly industrial smoke, and has sat around the 1.2% limit since this section began. Measured on 2026-10-11 and again on 2026-10-12 with six runs, three of the unmodified game and three with all of this section's replacements on, every pair compared in that shot: the unmodified game differs from itself by 0.88%, 1.47% and 1.70%; ours from ours by 0.51%, 1.06% and 1.33%; ours from the game's by 0.40% to 1.67% over the nine pairs. The runs fall into two groups whatever code they ran (one of the game's and two of ours are within 0.5% of each other, as are the other two of the game's and one of ours within 0.9%), so the smoke has two looks at that moment and the limit catches the pairs across them. The misses of that one shot reported in this section are this, and the other nineteen shots are the ones to read.

**What is left** (2026-10-19). A coverage run of the unmodified game (`diagnostics.coverage`, Big City with the camera workout, clouds and water effects on, 2026-10-11) reaches 248 functions in the effects' code (the manager, 0x00582000 to 0x00594000, and the effect classes, 0x005A8F80 to 0x005BA000). 165 of them are ours with this section's replacements: what runs for every effect and particle in every frame, the manager's sort, its clean-up after the draw, its start of a compound effect, its making of what a record holds, the makers with the constructors they call, the look-up of a name and the decal's description under it, the growth of its records with their destroy, the look-up by IDs, the starts by IDs and by name, the restart of those in use and the change of zoom, the store of compound descriptions, the descriptions' lists of held pointers and their vectors of records and texts, and some of an effect's set-up (a fifth function of the start's replacement, 0x005BFCD0, the decal's reset 0x00581F80, the compound effect's description 0x005BFF80 and its constructor 0x005C0150 are outside the two ranges counted). The other 83 are 70 of the manager's (making and finding effects, reading their descriptions, and its other lists; under the makers, the decal description's assign 0x005833F0; the record's assign 0x0058DBA0, ours too, is not among the 248, as Big City never assigns a record) and 13 of the effect classes' (a third class of effect, the letting go and delete at 0x005B9DD0, setters and small helpers). The manager's two other steps after the draw (0x0058E870, 0x0058EC40) and its height map's lines' draw before it (0x00586720) are not among the 248: no test city runs them (the draw is ours since 2026-10-25, `renderer.effects.heightmap_rest`, and the two steps too, `renderer.effects.flash` and `renderer.effects.shake`, all compared by forcing). Translated but never called in the three test cities, and so not in the tree: the move along a path (0x005B6600), the second class's in-view test (0x005AD890, which starts "camera_collision"), and found unreached: the starts at birth (0x005B84C0, ours by hand since 2026-10-25 and compared by forcing, `renderer.effects.birth_starts`), the step's last call (0x005B8020), the records' range copy (0x0058EF60), and under the making the makers of the third class and of kind 0x10 (0x00590EA0, 0x00591000) and the two calls for a remembered effect (0x005AC1C0, 0x005B2230).

**Tabled: a better water shader.** Dan asked on 2026-10-11 how hard a nicer water surface would be, and then tabled it in favour of feature parity. What was worked out, for when it is picked up: the water surface is drawn by our own pass (`renderer.terrain.water`, a mesh with one scrolling texture per zoom and per-vertex colours) through our D3D11 driver, which has one pixel shader for everything. An easy tier would be a second pixel shader used for that draw only: two layers of ripples moving in different directions, a soft sun glint and a colour shift with the view angle, and darkening with the depth the water's vertices already carry. It needs a time value passed to the shader and a tag on the water's draw, works only with our driver, and by the rule for improvements sits behind a setting with the original look as the default. Left out of that tier: foam where water meets land (the scene's depth read back) and reflections. Not checked: the farthest zoom, where the texture is tiny, and the region view, which draws its water another way. Nothing has been built.

## What the driver calls mean

Reference for writing a driver, from decompiling both stock drivers (vtables: DirectX 0x00AC4660, OpenGL 0x00AC36D0, `cIGZGDriver` at object + 0xC in both). The OpenGL driver is the plainest spec, since most methods are one GL call after a table lookup; the DirectX driver is what the game was tuned against and what the screenshot harness compares with (the OpenGL driver renders without terrain and with black boxes around trees on a current NVIDIA card, so it is no visual reference).

**The model is OpenGL 1.3 fixed function with `ARB_texture_env_combine`.** GD enums are indices into translation tables (OpenGL driver 0x00B12010-0x00B12238, DirectX driver 0x00AC44CC-0x00AC45F8); [gd.h](../src/core/gd/gd.h) lists them.

- **Window.** `SetVideoMode(mode, wndproc, ?, ?)` creates the driver's own ANSI window ("GDriverClass (DirectX)": `WS_OVERLAPPEDWINDOW | WS_VISIBLE` sized to the mode's client area for windowed modes, a popup otherwise), stores the driver in `GWLP_USERDATA` and sends every message to `wndproc`. Mode -1 destroys it. `SetGDriver` calls `SetVideoMode` itself when a mode is already set. Modes are `sGDMode` records of 0x34 bytes (index, width, height, bits per pixel, A/R/G/B masks, a full-screen flag at 0x20, then capabilities the game configures itself from; see "What the game asks the driver"); the DirectX driver lists 120 here, every desktop resolution twice (full screen and windowed).
- **Frames.** `Flush` presents: `glFlush` + `SwapBuffers`, or the DirectX driver's blit to the primary surface. The back buffer keeps its contents between frames, which the game relies on: it redraws only what changed. `Clear(mask)` takes 0x4000 colour, 0x1000 depth, 0x2000 stencil and clears the current viewport (a Direct3D 7 clear), with the values from `ClearColor`/`ClearDepth`/`ClearStencil`. `ColorMask` is a no-op in the DirectX driver.
- **Viewport.** GL coordinates, origin bottom left: the DirectX driver clamps to the surface and converts with `top = height - y - h`; `GetViewport` returns `{x, y, x + w, y + h}` in the same coordinates. Direct3D 7 viewports clip, so a viewport also acts as a scissor rectangle (the OpenGL driver sets `glScissor` with it).
- **Matrices.** `MatrixMode` 0 model-view, 1 projection; GL layout. The DirectX driver loads the model-view as the world matrix (view = identity) and rewrites the projection for Direct3D (depth range and half-pixel offset, which is why it reloads it on every viewport change).
- **Vertices.** `InterleavedArrays(format, stride, data)` only records the pointer (stride 0: the format's); `DrawArrays(prim, first, count)` and `DrawElements(prim, count, type, indices)` read from it, with unsigned byte, short or int indices. Formats are bit fields of per-element counts (position, normal, up to 3 colours, 1D-4D texture coordinates), with legacy IDs for common layouts ([vertex_format.h](../src/core/gd/vertex_format.h)). **Colours are D3DCOLOR (BGRA bytes)**: the OpenGL driver swaps red and blue in the caller's array before each draw and back after. Primitives: triangles, strips, fans, points, lines, line strips, quads, quad strips; the DirectX driver turns strips, fans and quads into indexed lists.
- **Colour.** `ColorMultiplier(r, g, b)` and `AlphaMultiplier(a)` switch on lighting with only an ambient light, and leave it on. In the DirectX driver (0x00883240, 0x00883310, 0x008833E0) the lit colour is the ambient material times the multiplier, where `EnableVertexColors(diffuse, specular)` picks the ambient material (vertex colour if `diffuse`, else white); the lit alpha is the diffuse material's, which `EnableVertexColors` sets to the vertex colour if `specular` is set and to the material otherwise, `AlphaMultiplier(a < 1)` sets to the material (alpha `a`), and `AlphaMultiplier(1)` sets back to the vertex colour if the last `specular` was set. (Ghidra's decompile of 0x008833E0 shows a constant for that argument; the disassembly reads `[esp+0x10]`, the second argument.) Cloud shadows depend on it: they redraw the terrain with `ColorMultiplier(0, 0, 0.2)`, `AlphaMultiplier(0)` and an alpha test of 0.22, so with the material's alpha of 0 the stock driver draws almost nothing there. Vertices without a colour use the material.
- **Textures.** `CreateTexture(format, w, h, levels, hints)` returns a handle (formats RGB5, RGB8, RGBA4, RGB5_A1, RGBA8, DXT1 with alpha, DXT3, DXT5); `LoadTextureLevel(texture, level, x, y, w, h, format, type, rowLength, pixels)` uploads (source formats RGB, RGBA, BGR, BGRA, alpha, luminance, luminance-alpha, DXT1/1A/3/5). `SetTexture(texture, stage)`, `GetTexture(stage)`; the GL-style `GenTextures`/`BindTexture`/`TexImage2D` exist but were not called in our runs. `TexParameter` 0-3 are min filter, mag filter, wrap S, wrap T, per stage.
